def RiemannHypothesis : Prop :=
∀ (s : ℂ), riemannZeta s = 0 → (¬∃ n, s = -2 * (↑n + 1)) → s ≠ 1 → s.re = 1 / 2
@@AXIOMS

@@END RiemannHypothesis

@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum : SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig →
  SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig → SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig :=
fun C₁ C₂ =>
  let __ZeroConfig := SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sumZ C₁ C₂;
  { toZeroConfig := __ZeroConfig, rhs := fun k => C₁.rhs k + C₂.rhs k, ef := ⋯, count := ⋯,
    target := C₁.target ∧ C₂.target, target_iff := ⋯ }
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum
@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum_ef
@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.Product @@LINE 111 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum_ef : ∀ (C₁ C₂ : SIDEExplicitFormula.Schema.WeilConfig) (k : ℝ → ℂ),
  ContDiff ℝ 2 k →
    HasCompactSupport k →
      (∀ (x : ℝ), k (-x) = k x) →
        ∑' (ρ : ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sumZ C₁ C₂).carrier),
            ↑((SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sumZ C₁ C₂).mult ↑ρ) * Zeta23.paperFT k (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ) =
          C₁.rhs k + C₂.rhs k
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum_ef' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.Product.sum_ef

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pair_algebra
@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.PairTerm @@LINE 349 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pair_algebra : ∀ (γ δ ε N S : ℝ) (F sl : ℂ),
  0 < γ →
    0 ≤ ε →
      0 < S →
        S ≤ N →
          ‖F‖ ≤ ε * N →
            ‖sl‖ ≤ γ * (1 + ε) * S →
              SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairEps ε δ γ ≤ SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairConst →
                (((↑δ ^ 2 + 2 * Complex.I * ↑γ * ↑δ) * (↑N + F) / 2) ^ 2 +
                      ((↑δ ^ 2 - 2 * Complex.I * ↑γ * ↑δ) * (↑N + (starRingEnd ℂ) F) / 2) ^ 2).re ≤
                  -(SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairConst - SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairEps ε δ γ) * δ ^ 2 * ‖sl‖ ^ 2 *
                    (N / S) ^ 2
@@AXIOMS

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pair_bound
@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.PairTerm @@LINE 435 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pair_bound : ∀ (γ₀ δ ε : ℝ) {φ : ℝ → ℝ},
  ContDiff ℝ 4 φ →
    HasCompactSupport φ →
      (∀ (x : ℝ), φ (-x) = φ x) →
        (∀ (x : ℝ), 0 ≤ φ x) →
          0 < ∫ (u : ℝ), φ u →
            0 < γ₀ →
              0 < δ →
                0 ≤ ε →
                  SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.farSmall γ₀ φ δ ε →
                    SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.farSmall γ₀ φ 0 ε →
                      SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairEps ε δ γ₀ ≤ SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairConst →
                        (SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairTwo γ₀ φ δ).re ≤
                          -(SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairConst - SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pairEps ε δ γ₀) * δ ^ 2 *
                              ‖SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.windowSlope γ₀ φ‖ ^ 2 *
                            SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.realizedGrowth φ δ ^ 2
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pair_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.B321.pair_bound

@@BEGIN SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiLimitExchange
@@CELL @@KIND def @@MODULE SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil @@LINE 359 @@CONSUMES [SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiCoeff,
 Zeta23.zetaZeroConfig] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiLimitExchange : ℕ → Prop :=
fun n =>
  ∀ (c : ℕ → ℝ) (fs : (m : ℕ) → ContDiffBump (c m)),
    (∀ (m : ℕ), c m + (fs m).rOut ≤ 0) →
      (∀ u < 0, Filter.Tendsto (fun m => ↑(fs m) u) Filter.atTop (nhds 1)) →
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun m =>
            ∑' (ρ : ↑Zeta23.zetaZeroConfig.carrier),
              ↑(Zeta23.zetaZeroConfig.mult ↑ρ) *
                Zeta23.paperFT (SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.truncMember n (fs m)) (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ))
          Filter.atTop (nhds ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiCoeff n))
@@AXIOMS
'SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiLimitExchange' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiLimitExchange

 Zeta23.zetaZeroConfig] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
def SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiLimitExchangeSym : ℕ → Prop :=

  Filter.Tendsto
    (fun δ =>
      ∑' (ρ : ↑Zeta23.zetaZeroConfig.carrier),
        ↑(Zeta23.zetaZeroConfig.mult ↑ρ) *
          Zeta23.paperFT (SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.symMember n δ) (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ))
    (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds ↑(SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.LiCoeff n))
@@AXIOMS

def SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.SymPairBound : ℕ → Prop :=

  ∃ C,

              0 < ρ.re →
                ρ.re < 1 →
                  |(Zeta23.paperFT (SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.symMember n δ) (Zeta23.gammaOf ρ)).re| ≤ C / ‖ρ‖ ^ 2
@@AXIOMS

theorem SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.integral_mul_cos_ibp : ∀ {k k' k'' : ℝ → ℝ} (a γ : ℝ),
  γ ≠ 0 →
    (∀ (x : ℝ), HasDerivAt k (k' x) x) →
      (∀ (x : ℝ), HasDerivAt k' (k'' x) x) →
        Continuous k'' →
          ∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..a, k u * Real.cos (γ * u) =
            k a * (Real.sin (γ * a) / γ) + (k' a * Real.cos (γ * a) - k' 0) / γ ^ 2 -
              (∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..a, k'' u * Real.cos (γ * u)) / γ ^ 2

  ∃ C,
    ∀ (δ : ℝ),

        δ ≤ 1 →
          ∀ (β : ℝ),
            0 ≤ β →
              β ≤ 1 →
                ∀ (γ : ℝ),
                  γ ≠ 0 →
                    |∫ (u : ℝ) in 0..δ / 2,
                          SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.wf δ u *
                              (SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.qf n β u - SIDEExplicitFormula.LiWeil.qf n β (-u)) *
                            Real.cos (γ * u)| ≤
                      C / γ ^ 2

theorem Zeta23.Analytic.rectangleIntegral'_mul_logDeriv : ∀ {f g : ℂ → ℂ} {z w : ℂ},
  z.re ≤ w.re →
    z.im ≤ w.im →
      AnalyticOnNhd ℂ f (z.Rectangle w) →
        AnalyticOnNhd ℂ g (z.Rectangle w) →
          (∀ s ∈ RectangleBorder z w, f s ≠ 0) →
            ∀ (Z : Finset ℂ),
              (∀ s ∈ z.Rectangle w, f s = 0 ↔ s ∈ Z) →
                ↑Z ⊆ z.Rectangle w →
                  RectangleIntegral' (fun s => g s * logDeriv f s) z w = ∑ ρ ∈ Z, ↑(analyticOrderNatAt f ρ) * g ρ
@@AXIOMS

def Zeta23.EF.EF_lit : Zeta23.ZeroConfig → Prop :=

  ∀ (k : ℝ → ℂ),
    ContDiff ℝ 2 k →
      HasCompactSupport k →
        (Summable fun ρ => ↑(Z.mult ↑ρ) * Zeta23.paperFT k (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ)) ∧
          ∑' (ρ : ↑Z.carrier), ↑(Z.mult ↑ρ) * Zeta23.paperFT k (Zeta23.gammaOf ↑ρ) = Zeta23.EF.literatureRHS k
@@AXIOMS

    AnalyticOnNhd ℂ f (Metric.closedBall s₀ R) →
      f s₀ ≠ 0 →
        2 ≤ B →
          (∀ w ∈ Metric.closedBall s₀ (24 / 25 * R), ‖f w‖ ≤ B * ‖f s₀‖) →
            ∃ Z,
              ↑Z = {ρ | ρ ∈ Metric.closedBall s₀ (22 / 25 * R) ∧ f ρ = 0} ∧
                ∑ ρ ∈ Z, ↑(analyticOrderNatAt f ρ) ≤ 1 / Real.log (24 / 25 / (22 / 25)) * Real.log B ∧
                  ∀ s ∈ Metric.closedBall s₀ (83 / 100 * R),
                    f s ≠ 0 → ‖logDeriv f s - ∑ ρ ∈ Z, ↑(analyticOrderNatAt f ρ) / (s - ρ)‖ ≤ 44795000 / R * Real.log B

@@BEGIN Zeta23.WeilEF.vertical_line_shift
@@CELL @@KIND theorem @@MODULE Zeta23.WeilEF.VerticalLine @@LINE 443 @@CONSUMES [] @@WATCHED []
@@STMT
theorem Zeta23.WeilEF.vertical_line_shift : ∀ {f : ℂ → ℂ} {a b : ℝ},
  a ≤ b →
    (∀ (s : ℂ), a ≤ s.re → s.re ≤ b → DifferentiableAt ℂ f s) →
      ∀ {φ : ℝ → ℝ},
        MeasureTheory.Integrable φ MeasureTheory.volume →
          (∀ (σ t : ℝ), a ≤ σ → σ ≤ b → ‖f (↑σ + ↑t * Complex.I)‖ ≤ φ t) →
            Filter.Tendsto φ Filter.atTop (nhds 0) →
              Filter.Tendsto φ Filter.atBot (nhds 0) →
                ∫ (t : ℝ), f (↑b + ↑t * Complex.I) = ∫ (t : ℝ), f (↑a + ↑t * Complex.I)
@@AXIOMS
'Zeta23.WeilEF.vertical_line_shift' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@@END Zeta23.WeilEF.vertical_line_shift
